## Check against known misses
- miss-001 (white play icon on white thumbnail): equivalents are lowvision-25 (status badges distinguished by hue only) and the low-vision leaves on selected-state overlays over white thumbnails; see ids starting `lowvision-`.
- miss-002 (batch with some failures, no partial state): equivalents are backend-3 (one silent video in a 20-video report), consumer-11 (report with 2 of 8 unusable exported as complete), contract-4 (denominator after an unusable video), and the qa-lead per-video-outcome leaves.

## Audit: expert-missing items (from H-audit-1.yaml, now covered by the fill where noted)
- L3 | Report output (Prompt/instructions empty) | no content contract: sections, one synthesis vs per-video sections, findings with severity, quote+timestamp per finding
- L3 | History entry detail, Done | no grounding rule: each claim cites video+timestamp, and an explicit 'not answerable from the selected videos' outcome instead of a confident answer
- L3 | 'ingested' state | spec does not say which modalities the agent reads (transcript only, frames, OCR, cursor), so on-screen questions on user-test screen recordings have no defined answer
- L3 | ingested transcript | no speaker diarisation rule (moderator vs participant, participant id per video), so 'N of M participants' counts and quote attribution are unreliable
- L3 | History entry detail | no feedback control (rate, flag a claim) and no pre-launch eval set or acceptance threshold for Report and Ask question quality
- L3 | History entry while Running | the entry replaces the removed loading page but has no streaming, progress or ETA rule for a 30-second answer vs a 40-minute Report
- L3 | Prompt / instructions field | no way to give study context (discussion guide, task list, research questions, participant segments) and no saved prompt templates for repeat studies
- L3 | Add video pop-up / selected videos list | no total selected duration, time or cost estimate, and no per-video time range (skip warm-up, analyse task 2 only)
- L3 | Report entry / export-or-copy-output | no 'ask about this report' bridge into Ask question and no highlight-clip export of cited moments for stakeholders
- L3 | Agent page as a whole | no success metrics or instrumentation (runs per user, Done/Failed rate, time to first output, follow-up rate, flag rate), so launch quality cannot be judged
